                      <h2>
              <strong>Get a head start on the spring semester with<strong> <a href="https://www.lccc.edu/winter-session/" target="_blank" style="color: white">Winter Courses. </a></strong>            </strong></h2><strong>
          
                      <div class="description-wrap cf">
              <p>Classes will be online. The five week semester begins on December 14, 2026 and ends on January 16, 2027.</p>

																		  <h3> We can help you with FAFSA.</h3>
												<p>Main Campus – Drop-in hours available Mon.-Thurs., 8 a.m. to 4:30 p.m.<br>
Tamaqua – Schedule an appointment Mon.-Thurs., 8 a.m. to 4:30 p.m.<br>
Allentown – FAFSA Completion Workshops are available Wed., 1 to 2 p.m. or by appointment.  Please <a href="https://studentservices.lccc.edu/ramonline" target="_blank" rel="noopener" tabindex="0">register online</a>.</p>

      <div class="jcr-post-message">
          This week is Hunger and Homelessness Awareness Week, and we'd like to highlight LCCC's Cougar Cabinet. 💙

At LCCC, we believe supporting students goes beyond the classroom. The Cougar Cabinet provides students with access to food, hygiene, and health products at each campus location.
